[L042][WP-02][P1][2026-07-02][A5]
FINDING: section 2 of rehydrations/policy/enforceable_by_architecture.md (draft v3) asserts that R_max = (C_S + C_M)/H(X) < 1 holds under the two stated preconditions. Neither precondition constrains C_S + C_M relative to H(X); the inequality is a system-specific numerical condition, not a consequence of the architecture. Section 3's definition of t* presupposes R(t) can reach one with the preconditions intact, so the artifact is also internally inconsistent on this point. BLOCKING at P1.
EVIDENCE: artifact section 2 read against Preconditions 1 and 2; section 3 (definition of t*). Full analysis at reviews/pc_reviews/WP-02_review_1.md, weakness 1.
PROPOSED: restate the guarantee: under the two preconditions the error-floor relation P_e >= 1 - R_max holds; when additionally C_S + C_M < H(X), a measurable and declarable condition, R_max < 1 and reconstruction in the stated sense is impossible against the stated adversary class. Carry the correction through section 3 and recommendation 2. A2 owns the fix; if the extraction itself carries the conflation, escalate CANON-LEVEL.

[L123][OBSERVERS/CTR-OBS-01][verification+inventory][2026-07-10][A3 (per L121(a); serialised by A0)]
FINDING: CTR-OBS-01 VERIFIED at the seat from first principles (Cover-Thomas identities; the interaction identity derived both sides to co-information): under Precondition 1 the joint leakage is SUBADDITIVE — I(X;Y_S,Y_M) = I(X;Y_S) + I(X;Y_M) - I(Y_S;Y_M) — with equality iff the outputs are also marginally independent. "Holds exactly when Precondition 1" is wrong in BOTH directions (P1 does not give equality; equality does not need P1, witness Y_S=Y_M=W independent of X). THE FLOOR SURVIVES under exactly Preconditions 1+2, conservatively: the capacity-sum numerator upper-bounds true joint information, and the true floor sits HIGHER by the redundancy term — the observer's critique tightens the ceiling favourably, as the disposition ledger's amendment claimed. The general failure I(X;Y_M|Y_S) <= C_M (xor witness) auto-resolves under P1; the composition threat enters through I(Y_S;Y_M|X), not the redundancy term. BLAST-RADIUS INVENTORY: 10 EXACT-wording sites (+3 ambiguous), 12+ BOUND sites all conformant. Pipeline-touchable EXACT sites = TWO ONLY (E1-C04 at extractions/E1-amnesia-gap.md:50; grant_edition.md:133 inheriting it verbatim). Canon EXACT sites (register process only): spec §14.7/§16 x2, privacy_value_v6:132, dualprivacy_researchpaper_v6:30, pvm_v6_compressed:112/:235, V5.4 spec :634 (superseded — flag not edit); adjacent ring incl. the L113(b) hearthold instance. THE THREE AWAITING-P4 PAPERS NEED NOTHING: WP-07 Thm 3.1 and WP-04 Prop 1(a) already state the inequality with equality-iff-marginal-independence and prove via the interaction identity; per-theorem audit confirms NO PROOF uses exact additivity; WP-27 carries no additivity content. NEW FINDING flagged separately: pvm_v6_compressed.md:112 also carries an unswept pre-L044 conflation (CI implies R_max<1) — the L044 execution missed the compressed edition. FENCE recorded: the deficit-condition "holds exactly when C_S+C_M<H(X)" is a genuine definitional iff and stays OUT of any fix.
EVIDENCE: reviews/CTR-OBS-01_verification_and_inventory.md (derivation + per-site table + per-theorem audit); chronicles/2026-07-10_a3-ctr-obs-01.md; the drafts and canon surfaces at the cited lines (read-only).

[L130][WP-14][canon-figure-rebuild / CANON-LEVEL][2026-07-14][A12 (E4-C04 rebuild obligation discharged; GR-10 escalation, not resolution)]
FINDING: The two per-person value-gap figures in the spec 30 canonical-figures table do NOT survive the WP-14 tier-A rebuild as asserted facts. (a) 678x ("present-day per-person data-value gap"): not point-identified. The ratio form V_potential/V_current is ill-posed when the subject's captured value V_current tends to zero (a division artefact, not a measurement); value is aggregation-dependent, so any single ratio silently fixes dataset composition, model class, and counterfactual. Replaced by a bounded surplus-appropriation share s in [0,1] (extraction regime [0,0.05]; sovereign-regime projection [0.3,0.7]) and a non-identified super-additive aggregation wedge w (order 10^1..10^3, sign and super-additivity asserted only), both range-valued, with a named market mechanism (consent-interface bargaining setting s; subject-side aggregator capturing w; non-transferable proof-of-practice resource as the Sybil-resistant quality signal, register conjecture C42). The value 678 is not reproduced. (b) 31,000x ("accessible-volume value gap"): does not survive as a valuation ratio. It was already reframed IN THE CANON (v4-economics 219; whitepaper 577) from an arithmetic ratio to a non-arithmetic "difference in accessible volume on the same manifold... topology, not arithmetic". A non-arithmetic quantity is not a valuation figure; at tier A it is retired as a number and replaced by an unquantified structural claim (an extraction architecture cannot activate subject-side protection without degrading the observation its revenue depends on). No fiat figure appears anywhere in the rebuild (GR-3/L057); all magnitudes are dimensionless shares, wedges, and ranges. SECOND FINDING (E4-C02, re-flag): the whitepaper six-capital enumeration (Financial, Manufactured, Natural, Human, Social, Cultural) diverges from the IIRC International <IR> Framework six capitals (Financial, Manufactured, Intellectual, Human, Social and Relationship, Natural); corrected in the rebuild and skeleton before tier-A reuse; the seventh-capital thesis (C55, architectural, occupied) is unaffected.
EVIDENCE: rehydrations/academic/weis_valuation_methodology_note.md (the rebuild, Sections 1-9); rehydrations/academic/weis_seventh_capital_skeleton.md (WP-14 section map); chronicles/2026-07-14_a12_wp14_rebuild.md; spec 30 canonical-figures table; v4-economics 219; E4-seventh-capital c02/c04.
PROPOSED: (a) A0 to file the CANON-LEVEL note for the register process: 678x and 31,000x do not survive the WP-14 tier-A rebuild and are replaced by the share/wedge methodology and the de-numbered structural claim respectively; the IIRC six-capital enumeration in the whitepaper is incorrect against the external source. (b) The register process, not A12, decides what the canon surfaces do (GR-10); A12 does not resolve canon. (c) A4 to add the ten data-markets bib entries (note Section 8) before the prose port. (d) A3 to review the proposed M(u,y) operationalisation (note Section 5). (e) A5 economist-referee pass, target MAJOR-at-worst.

[L131][WP-14][P1-review-1][2026-07-14][A5 economist-referee persona (WEIS PC)]
FINDING: WP-14 (rehydrations/academic/weis_seventh_capital.md) review 1 = MAJOR REVISION: 0 BLOCKING, 5 MAJOR (M1-M5), 7 MINOR (m1-m7). No desk-reject grounds; clears the pipeline MAJOR-at-worst target. GR-DISCIPLINE CLEAN under hostile reading: GR-3 (no 678x/31,000x/70:1/74x/fiat anywhere; only residual quantitative token is "roughly one order of magnitude", see M4), GR-7 (every R(t) invocation carries A1 non-collusion + A2 fixed-adversary-class + A3 time-indexing in-passage; P4 explicitly quarantines and refuses to import the source's retired "mathematical guarantees collapse" sentence), GR-8 (Section 6 gives negatives equal prominence, reporting-commitment present) all satisfied; conjectures honestly labelled at or below extraction status (C55 stated as thesis-with-proof-obligation not settled; C42/C95/E4-C07 carried as conjectures; M(u,y) labelled proposal; tier multipliers illustrative). The defects are contribution-weight and completeness, not honesty. MAJOR-1 (contribution, Section 4): the central economic proposition P1 (interface sets the appropriation share) is NOT derived — the basis text offers two different bargaining models as interchangeable then defers the derivation to a "proof obligation"; the whole Results section is deferred IOUs; a WEIS economist needs at least one self-contained worked result and P1 needs no conjecture to prove. MAJOR-2 (novelty, Section 2): the claimed-novel "third position" (data as subject-owned capital) restates the personal-data-as-property literature (Laudon 1996, Samuelson 2000, Schwartz 2004 Harv.L.Rev., Purtova, Prins) without citing it, and does not answer that literature's standing objections to propertization (alienability defeats protection, thin markets, regressivity). MAJOR-3 (foundation, Sections 3/6): the durability/term-structure argument rests entirely on imported WP-07 which a referee cannot evaluate; "the asset has a term structure" is drawn from the mere existence of R(t) whose shape/timescale the paper never states — term structure needs the schedule. MAJOR-4 (rigor regression, Section 5.1): "roughly one order of magnitude" share-shift asserted in prose with NO interval on either endpoint (table cells read only "near zero"/"interior"/"small integer multiple"); the draft dropped the A12-note ranges ([0,0.05]/[0.3,0.7]/[2,6]/10^1..10^3) and kept the 10x conclusion, making the paper LESS quantitatively disciplined than its own methodology substrate; a smuggled semi-quantitative residue of the retired 678x. MAJOR-5 (framing legibility): "seventh capital" indexes off the IIRC integrated-reporting six-capitals frame, a corporate-reporting construct with no economics-of-capital standing; the ordinal reads as branding to the target audience. MINORS: m1 M(u,y) proposed then never used (cut or use); m2 A1 (assumed non-collusion) vs P4 (incentive-compatible non-collusion) possibly circular/redundant and never connected — P4 plausibly discharges A1 and that synthesis is missed; m3 P4 no-exchange-pool assumption fragile to a shadow exchange rate, undefended; m4 "super-additive" conflated with "w>1", "bounded" stated without a bound; m5 WTA/WTP over-read as "signature of appropriation"; m6 near-zero extraction share ignores the value of the free service (zero-price side of a two-sided market); m7 subject-side aggregator = MID/data-cooperative instrument, under-attributed beyond Arrieta-Ibarra (cite Delacroix & Lawrence 2019 and kin). SINGLE HIGHEST-LEVERAGE CHANGE: derive P1 in full (one bargaining model, explicit disagreement/outside-option points per interface, share->0 vs interior share as comparative statics) — converts the paper from framing+methodology+promises to framing+methodology+a proven mechanism.
EVIDENCE: reviews/pc_reviews/WP-14_A5_economist_2026-07-14.md (summary, three strengths, M1-M5 + m1-m7 with anchors, desk-reject verdict, single-change recommendation); the draft at rehydrations/academic/weis_seventh_capital.md; the A12 methodology note weis_valuation_methodology_note.md (the dropped ranges of M4); E4-seventh-capital extraction (status cross-check for over-claim test); chronicles/2026-07-14_a5_wp14_review.md.
PROPOSED: (a) A2/A3 revision card addressing M1 first (derive P1), then M2-M5 and the seven minors per the review's what-satisfies text; A3 owns the P1 formal model and the M(u,y) disposition (m1); (b) M2's property-literature and m7's data-cooperative additions route to A4 as bib work; (c) M3 gates on WP-07 being a real citable artifact at submission and on the R(t) schedule being stateable enough to license the term-structure claim; (d) re-review after revision-1 for the MAJOR-to-accept-class move.

[L133][WP-14][valuation-wedge-tightening / CANON-LEVEL][2026-07-15][A12 (Run-3 wedge/observer-capture tightening on the v2 rebuild; GR-10 escalation, not resolution)]
FINDING: The Run-3 tightening of the valuation rebuild (rehydrations/academic/weis_valuation_methodology_v2_empirical.md) narrows the two objects L132 left wide, and in doing so CORRECTS v2's characterisation of the aggregation wedge. (1) OBSERVER CAPTURE is now DATA-ATTRIBUTABLE, not gross: the targeting-premium fraction x is bracketed from external literature (E39-E45) at x in [0.04, 0.52], central band [0.2, 0.4], policy-clean midpoint ~0.20 (Kraft et al. ATT aggregate). The proxy carried downstream is x x ARPU = $8.92-$17.84/yr central (worldwide E1 $44.60), not the gross $44.60; gross is retired as the data proxy, kept only as an upper bound. The evidence splits into an AGGREGATE family (~0.04 publisher-realized E39; ~0.18-0.23 controlled price regression E42; ~0.20 ATT revenue loss E41; ~0.27 advertiser-side implied from +37% CPA E43 = the correct family for scaling capture) and a MARGINAL family (~0.50-0.52 opt-out/auction premium E40, E41, E44 = a selection-conditional stress that OVERSTATES the aggregate). NEGATIVE (GR-8): the ~13x divergence between the independent E39 (~4%) and Google's own E44 (~52%) is unresolved; the largest marginal figures carry commercial conflicts (Google, Meta-affiliated, GumGum E45 dropped UNVERIFIED). (2) THE WEDGE is split and its super-additive claim COLLAPSES. v2 reported a single w order 10^1-10^5 whose top divided the observer's GROSS ANNUAL flow by a ONE-TIME broker record price (flow/stock boundary, L132). Run-3 (i) rebuilds the top STOCK/STOCK (lifetime data-attributable capture $107-$214 = x x $44.60/yr x 12yr, over one-time targeted broker record $0.0021 E20, worldwide central) = 5.1x10^4 to 1.0x10^5, removing the flow/stock crossing and shaving the central top from v2's ~4.5x10^5 to ~1x10^5; and (ii) applies returns-to-scale evidence (E46-E50: Bajari et al. NBER WP 24334 forecast error at 1/sqrt(N)+1/sqrt(T) with variety N flat-to-negative and marginal value ->0; de Fortuny et al. diminishing past 500M; Varian's 99.9% Google discard; Neumann et al. two-attribute broker profiling ~24% accuracy) to establish that the SUPER-ADDITIVE aggregation component w_super is order 10^0-10^1 and SATURATES. The observer-to-broker gap of order 10^4-10^5 is therefore NOT super-additive aggregation value; it is atomisation discount + market-position rent + lifetime accumulation. v2's "w is super-additive, order 10^1-10^5" is WITHDRAWN: the super-additive part is order 10^0-10^1; the large gap is rent, not aggregation. Both remain non-identified as points (the rent gap moves with region/tenure/denominator, stress ~1x10^4 to ~2x10^6). UNTOUCHED (bear-check applied): s-share (4.1), WTA:WTP ratio (4.2), no-triangulation headline (5); the fraction bears on s only through 1/x (~2.5-5x) which does not change s's order. No fiat value of privacy asserted (GR-3/L057 held); every model output dimensionless.
EVIDENCE: rehydrations/academic/weis_valuation_methodology_v2_empirical.md (Sections 0 verdict finding 3 + Run-3 note, 1, 2.5 new E39-E50, 3 table, 4.3 rewritten, 4.4 new, 5, 8 patched, 9 handoff); chronicles/2026-07-15_a12_wedge_tightening.md; L132 (the flow/stock finding this discharges); E4-seventh-capital extraction.
PROPOSED: (a) A0 to file the CANON-LEVEL note for the register process: the wedge is not super-additive at order 10^3+; its super-additive component is order 10^0-10^1 and saturates (returns-to-scale), and the large observer-to-broker gap is rent, not aggregation. This SUPERSEDES the wedge characterisation in L130 (10^1-10^3) and L132 (10^1-10^5). The register process, not A12, decides the canon surface (GR-10). (b) A4 to add the ten Run-3 bib entries E39-E50 (Section 9 handoff), marking E44 vendor-conflict and E45 UNVERIFIED/dropped. This ruling resolves the meta-evolution (the compounding-vs-rent tension of L130/L132/L134) by adoption rather than escalation, and the soil figure unifies all four amendments: (1) RENTIER NOT COMPOUNDING (L134) — soil yields rent by position, marginal product of one more raw record ~ 0 (Bajari 2019 flat-N, Varian 2014 subsample, Neumann 2019 near-chance); the gap is atomisation discount + market-position rent + lifetime accumulation, NOT super-additive aggregation; compounding survives ONLY as reputational fertility (conjecture, P5). (2) VALUE IS THE APPROPRIATION SHARE THE INTERFACE SETS, NOT A FIGURE (L130/L132) — headline multiples (678x per-person, 31,000x accessible-volume) do not survive as asserted facts (ratio ill-posed as V_current->0; 31,000x already non-arithmetic in canon; convergence refuted); value = bounded surplus-appropriation share, SPE of the consent-interface bargaining game, share-shift as a difference of intervals. (4) STRUCTURAL INALIENABILITY BY NON-RECONSTRUCTION is the bridge where the security guarantee BECOMES the ownability (lease the harvest, not the land); candidate bridge conjecture. Plus the epistemic evolution (L137/L138): the model earns belief by narrowing and by knowing which neighbour owns each broad move (DP-markets, propertisation, data-as-labour, Mosca).

[L157][E8-C14 / g42 / ceremony-specs][the claimed hash divergence REFUTED by direct byte-level test + the spec-copy variant map][2026-07-18][A0 verification pass under the "solve the bugs" directive; method lesson filed]
FINDING: (1) E8-C14 IS REFUTED. The L156 claim that game42's hash.js diverges from the byte-exact Python reference on non-ASCII content rested on a sweep reader's assertion that "JSON.stringify escapes non-ASCII to backslash-u sequences". Direct empirical test (Python json.dumps ensure_ascii=False vs Node JSON.stringify, byte-level hex comparison over six vectors: accented Latin, CJK, an astral-plane emoji, control character 0x1f, em-dash, escape-bearing strings) shows the two encoders are BYTE-IDENTICAL on every vector. JSON.stringify does not escape printable non-ASCII; both sides emit raw UTF-8. The port is faithful for all valid Unicode. Sole residual divergence: lone surrogates (invalid Unicode) — ES2019 well-formed stringify escapes them, Python raises on UTF-8 encode; resolution = keep payloads valid Unicode, no code change. EXECUTED: the L156 warning comment in hash.js replaced with an accurate conformance note carrying the test vectors; E8-C14 re-typed Refuted-on-verification; the E8-C41 evidence bound STRENGTHENED (the cross-implementation seal byte-match now generalises beyond ASCII payloads). INDEPENDENT CONFIRMATION: the E11 kappa-implementation sweep, running concurrently and blind to this test, reports the same fact for the soulbis implementations ("no custom string escaper anywhere... resolves unambiguously to raw UTF-8"). METHOD LESSON (feeds E10/WP-27, the D2 class): the sweep reader's QUOTES were verbatim-faithful and its behavioral claim built on them was still false — quote fidelity does not verify behavioral claims about code; execution does. The cycle's Phase 3 discipline gains a rule: any sweep claim about CODE BEHAVIOR (as opposed to text content) is verified by running it before it enters an extraction as CONTESTED, let alone as fact. A1 self-critique: I filed E8-C14 and placed a warning comment in a reference implementation on an unexecuted claim — the warning itself was the defect.
(2) SPEC-COPY VARIANT MAP (closes the L156 future-touch): three copies of ceremony-engine-spec-v1_1.md exist and all three differ. DOCS (343 lines): 478-node graph figure, Act XXXI = "Protocol / Amnesia Protocol". MASTER (343 lines): identical to DOCS except the STALE 119-node figure — 5 diff lines total. FORGE (400 lines): 119-node figure, Act XXXI = "Delegation / The First Delegation", PLUS a unique 57-line section 8.5 "Runecraft Protocol (OPERATIONAL)" (dual Ed25519 keypairs bound to one blade, Mage key held in localStorage / Swordsman key burned) that exists in NEITHER sibling. This explains E8-C38's 478-vs-119: the 119 figure is the stale copies, not a live count (the E11 spellweb sweep will report the actual current number). DISPOSITION PROPOSED (First Person): name one copy canonical (DOCS is freshest), fold FORGE's unique 8.5 runecraft section into it, retire or pointer-stub the other two; until then no artifact cites a graph count from any copy without a date.
EVIDENCE: scratchpad enc_test.py/enc_test.js outputs (hex-identical x6); game42/src/hash.js conformance note (replaces the withdrawn warning); extractions/E8-ceremony-governance.md E8-C14 + contested table + E8-C41 (re-typed); the three-way diff (engine_spec_diff.txt in session scratchpad); the E11 kappa sweep transcript.

Nothing committed/pushed.

[L158][swordsman-blade / mages-spell / spellweb / ceremony-specs][the E11 sweeps' code findings VERIFIED by direct read; one real bug fixed, three honest boundaries recorded][2026-07-18][A0 under the "solve the bugs" directive; every code claim execution/read-verified per the L157 rule]
FINDING: The dual-extension and spellweb sweeps surfaced code-behavior claims; per the L157 execution-verification rule each was checked directly before action. RESULTS: (1) REAL BUG FIXED - the ECDH handshake round-trip was structurally broken in BOTH extensions. performKeyExchange (swordsman :123-160, mage :152-189) sends its public JWK and gates shared-secret derivation on response?.publicKey, but the KEY_EXCHANGE handler on each side replied sendResponse({acknowledged:true}) with NO publicKey (swordsman :232-235, mage :277-280) - so the initiator branch never ran and sharedSecret stayed null on BOTH sides. The message type carries publicKey (shared/types/channel.ts :136-139) so the responder had the initiator's key available and simply discarded it. FIX: both KEY_EXCHANGE handlers now generate an ephemeral P-256 pair, importKey the sender's JWK, deriveKey the AES-GCM-256 shared secret, and return their own public JWK in the response (dated comment L158 in both files). The shared/types contract is unchanged (response shape was untyped acknowledge). Both repos' shared/ trees stay byte-identical (the AETHER.md diff -rq discipline holds - the fix touches src/background/index.ts only, which legitimately differs between repos). NOTE the fix makes the handshake COMPLETE, not USED: sharedSecret is still never consumed (no crypto.subtle.encrypt/decrypt anywhere; the ceremony channel is plaintext) - that is a separate, larger deployment gap left for First Person, recorded honestly in E11, not silently expanded. (2) VERIFIED-AND-LEFT (honest boundaries, not bugs to fix): signSpell returns the literal 'placeholder_signature' with a // TODO (swordsman :287+); receivers never crypto.subtle.verify; extension IDs are 'MAGE_EXTENSION_ID_PLACEHOLDER'/'SWORDSMAN_...' so cross-extension sendMessage cannot resolve until Web Store publishing - all three are pre-ship states the docs already flag (AETHER/INTERFACE_CONTRACT), correctly E11 Design-assumption/deployment-boundary material, NOT defects to patch. (3) the additive-leakage GUARANTEE I(X;Y_S,Y_M)=I(X;Y_S)+I(X;Y_M) is prose with NO code measuring it and the channels are in fact coupled (shared chrome.storage, message exchange) - E11 flags it as an unmeasured claim (GR-8), the honesty item of the extension pair. (4) SPELLWEB LIVE COUNTS (settles E8-C38 fully): the running graph is 807 nodes / 1738 edges from src/data/nodes.ts + edges.ts (the 4-array edge split is a TS compiler workaround, not a data-model split) - neither 478 nor 119 nor the dead 16-node public/spellweb/nodes.json is live; the DOCS engine-spec 478 figure dated as a snapshot pointing at nodes.ts as authority. (5) FOG-OF-WAR retirement REFINED at both artefact-spec copies: the render path still EXISTS in code (graph.ts :302-307, SpellWeb.tsx :1032) and is inert only because no canonical node carries hiddenUntilWitness - retirement was by data de-population, not code removal; the notes now say so (the earlier L154 "legacy metadata, no display effect" was slightly wrong about the code - corrected). (6) spellweb import confirmed trust-on-ingest (commitmentVerified:true "Trust the import", regex frontmatter+body parse, blade_signature/hash emitted-but-ignored) - exactly the format-spec S8 "Phase 3" deferral, clean E11 material; SPELLWEB_INTEGRATION_SPEC_v2.md is a superseded Next.js design the Vite/D3 build never followed (its "must not modify" file list points at files that do not exist) - E11 records the spec as non-conformant-to-build.
EVIDENCE: swordsman-blade/src/background/index.ts + mages-spell/src/background/index.ts (KEY_EXCHANGE handlers, fixed + dated); direct reads of :123-160/:232-235 both repos + channel.ts :136-139; the four E11 sweep transcripts; 09-spellweb-artefact-md-format.md x2 (fog notes refined); ceremony-engine-spec-v1_1.md DOCS copy (figure dated); L157 (the rule this pass follows).
PROPOSED: (a) First-Person queue updated: DROP the hash.js item (L157, refuted); ADD nothing new that's blocking - the ECDH fix is applied, the signing-stub / placeholder-ID / unused-shared-secret / additive-leakage-measurement items are all pre-ship deployment gaps that E11 records rather than defects needing a ruling. (b) Optional First-Person builds if desired: consume sharedSecret to encrypt the ceremony channel; implement signSpell + receiver verify; these are real Phase-3 work, scoped in E11. (c) the 807/1738 live counts become the citable figures; the three spec-copy variants still want the canonicalization disposition (L157). Nothing committed/pushed. tsc unavailable in both extension repos (no local typescript); the edits are type-compatible by construction (response gains an optional publicKey field, initiator already reads it).

[L162][WP-11a / WP-11 / RPP novelty framing][FIRST-PERSON RULING: RPP's novelty is prompt-injection-as-a-feature + proverbial-surface-over-hard-crypto, NOT comprehension-as-auth; WP-11a re-scouted and revised][2026-07-18][First Person ruling; A10 re-scout + revision]
FINDING: The First Person ruled that WP-11a pass 1 tested the WRONG novelty axis. RPP is not "comprehension as authentication" (the axis E3's N1-N5 candidates framed, which pass 1 correctly found all-MIRAGE/crowded); its novelty is three things pass 1 never tested: (M1) PROMPT INJECTION USED AS A CONSTRUCTIVE MECHANISM — a directive embedded in content that FORCES the reader/model to author a relationship-proverb as a precondition of use; the point is NOT unforgeability (a model CAN perform the act, fine) but compelling the cognitive act by injection-as-design ("the way a prompt injection works is you force the thinking"); (M2) a human-readable PROVERBIAL SURFACE OVER hard crypto/hashes — RPP is NOT the security layer, it is the recovery/binding interface; (M3) RELATIONAL/EPISODIC-MEMORY-backed recovery material that beats random-word mnemonics on recall ("plays into relational memory better than random words"). understanding-as-key is the SYSTEM; RPP is its mechanism; it is brain-wallet-SHAPED but the security rests on the hard-crypto substrate, not the proverb's entropy. PASS-2 RE-SCOUT (one general-purpose scout, interrupted by process exit then resumed): M1 DEFENSIBLE + CLEANEST — the prompt-injection literature is ~99% threat-framed (Greshake, Perez-Ribeiro, OWASP LLM01); the only constructive uses are ADVERSARIAL-DEFENSIVE (Tracebit "context bombs" 2026-07-14, the mandatory carve-out; canary/tripwire honeypots) or SELF-STEERING CoT (Kojima 2022, steers the operator's own model, not a directive in third-party content to gate a reader); NO prior work uses cooperative injection to compel a legitimate reader's generative act as a use-precondition. M2 DEFENSIBLE IFF BINDING-NOT-DERIVING — safe vs pure ENCODING layers (BIP-39, PGP word list, mnemonikey, what3words: bits->words, zero comprehension) but the DECIDER is TDCommons defensive publication article 9969 (VERIFIED this session: DERIVES a private key FROM a single narrative via NLP->embedding->LSH->fuzzy-extractor->deterministic key, reword-tolerant) which occupies the semantic-layer-over-crypto cell in the DERIVE case; M2 survives ONLY if understanding-as-key BINDS the proverb to pre-existing hard key material (entropy in the hard crypto) rather than DERIVES key material from it (entropy in the proverb, brain-wallet, TDCommons collision) — THIS IS A FIRST-PERSON ARCHITECTURE QUESTION, filed like the E6-C06 seam, not resolved by A10. M3 PREMISE TEXTBOOK (generation effect Slamecka-Graf 1978, levels-of-processing Craik-Lockhart 1972 — cannot claim the recall EFFECT), systems-synthesis (relational material AS crypto recovery material beating random mnemonics) UNRESOLVED pending a SOUPS/USEC sweep NOT yet run. HONESTY OBLIGATIONS RECLASSIFIED: the brain-wallet critique splits into memorability (M3 solves) + entropy (hard crypto solves IF bind; applies IF derive) — conditional on the M2 ruling, not a standing liability; AI-fakes-comprehension DISSOLVES (unforgeability was never the claim; forcing the act is the mechanism; "unforgeable" dropped); compression=comprehension STANDS but discharged by the N3 demotion. NET: the corrected framing carries ONE conditional honesty item (derive-case entropy) instead of three standing liabilities, and one CLEAN novel leg (M1) the first sweep never tested. WP-11a revised: §0 corrected-framing SUPERSEDES the §1-§2 auth-primitive analysis (retained as evidence for what NOT to pitch); honesty section reclassified; verdict rewritten around M1 + N2 (bilateral non-equality pair, now a load-bearing distinguisher vs TDCommons single-narrative); §4b outstanding-verification added. 4 checks PASS.

PROPOSED: (a) THE PIPELINE LESSON (feeds E10/WP-27): a prior-art sweep is only as good as the novelty FRAMING it is handed; pass 1 was adversarially correct about the WRONG axis (the auth-primitive framing came from E3's N-candidates); the A10 discipline held (no flattery) but the axis needs First-Person supply when the extraction's framing undersells the mechanism. Consider an A10 pre-step: confirm the novelty AXIS with the First Person before scouting. (b) WP-11's lead is M1 (cooperative injection), not a comprehension credential; WP-11 CANNOT be drafted until the M2 derive-vs-bind ruling. [L163][WP-11 / WP-11a / RPP architecture][GATE G-RPP-ARCH RULED: Option C (derive-from-the-interleaved-pair) + the tunable visibility ratio; WP-11 unblocked][2026-07-18][First Person ruling ("do option c... use the interleaved visibility ratio in this knowledge sharing"); A0 executing]
FINDING: The First Person ruled the derive-vs-bind gate (G-RPP-ARCH, opened L162): OPTION C. RPP's proverb is neither a single-narrative seed (Option A, brain-wallet, TDCommons-9969 collision) nor a pure pointer over an independent key (Option B, needs a bolted-on second factor); it is ONE HALF OF A TWO-PARTY DERIVATION — the trust object is the interleaved pair κ = hash(interleave(P_A, P_B)), split across two parties, no single party ever holding the whole. PLUS the First Person ruled a design element: the two proverbs combine by the INTERLEAVED inscription path (the third of symmetric/asymmetric/interleaved, E3-C10) parameterized by a DISCLOSURE RATIO σ ∈ [0,1] (E3-C22 disclosure budget): the public anchor carries σ of the interleaved pair, the two parties jointly hold the (1−σ) private fraction; σ is a tunable knob on the SHARED KNOWLEDGE, trading anchor-verifiability against how much of the joint understanding stays between the two parties. CONSEQUENCES: (1) the brain-wallet critique is DISCHARGED, not merely reclassified — Option C removes the target the critique needs (there is no single-party low-entropy secret to guess); it converts into a positive DEFENSE ("unlike single-narrative brain wallets and TDCommons-9969, the recoverable secret is never held by nor derivable from one party") and the threat model correctly becomes MALICIOUS-COUNTERPARTY, not outside-guesser (a co-participant holds one half by construction — the stronger, honest framing). (2) M2 novelty is now CLEAN vs TDCommons-9969: TDCommons is single-narrative/passive-input/no-disclosure-knob; RPP is bilateral + injection-forced + tunable interleaved σ. (3) the security argument for A3 is now well-posed: state security as a function of the two-party split and (1−σ); the disclosure/security trade is the σ relation. (4) DEPLOYMENT HONESTY (E3-C32): the interleaved path + σ budget are DESIGN-layer (the deployed system uses the asymmetric default + stratum-derived visibility); the φ sweet-spots for σ (0.382/0.5/0.618) are register C54 conjectural values, cited as candidate parameters not asserted optima. WP-11a REVISED: §0 M2 bullet records the ruling + interleaved-σ; §3 honesty-obligation-1 rewritten from conditional-liability to DISCHARGED-and-now-a-defense; §4 verdict rebuilt on M1 + the Option-C interleaved bilateral construction; §4b marks G-RPP-ARCH ruled. 4 checks PASS. MANIFEST: WP-11 gate cleared (G-RPP-ARCH → null), status novelty-reframed-blocked → novelty-established, next chain step A6.
EVIDENCE: rehydrations/academic/WP-11a_prior_art.md (§0 M2 ruled, §3 obligation-1 discharged, §4 verdict, §4b); the First-Person messages 2026-07-18 ("do option c i think and we can use the interleafed visibility ratio in this knowledge sharing"); the three inscription modes (E3-C10), the σ disclosure budget (E3-C22, design-layer per E3-C32), the φ disclosure-ratio conjecture (register C54); TDCommons-9969 (verified single-narrative derive) as the distinguished neighbour.
PROPOSED: (a) WP-11 is UNBLOCKED to A6 (benchmark engineer): the prereg (prereg_required, inheriting E3-C34's experimental-method precedent) + the benchmark that would actually MEASURE the M1 forcing claim and the co-participant-reconstruction threat. Then A3 formalises security as f(split, 1−σ); then A5-pc. (b) WIDENING-SWEEP CANDIDATE: the interleaved-path + σ-budget mechanism (E3-C10/C22) is now ruled load-bearing for a paper; an E3 widening sweep may promote it from design-note to a first-class extraction claim, and re-type E3's N-framing (auth-primitive) as superseded by the injection-mechanism framing (the L162 lesson) — a maintenance-mode widening, not a rebuild. (c) A4: TDCommons-9969 full text (authenticated fetch) + the 3 memory DOIs. (d) next scout: the SOUPS/USEC M3 usable-security sweep. (e) the σ disclosure-budget being DESIGN-not-deployed (E3-C32) is the one honesty line WP-11 carries about the mechanism's maturity. Nothing committed/pushed.

[L164][WP-11 / A6][RPP adversarial benchmark DESIGN + pre-registration drafted; 3 falsifiable hypotheses + the σ-frontier][2026-07-18][A6 benchmark engineer]
FINDING: A6 designed the WP-11 experiment against the RULED novelty (L162/L163), not the superseded auth-primitive framing. Deliverable: rehydrations/academic/rpp_adversarial_prereg.md (DESIGN v1, PRE-REGISTRATION DRAFT, 4 checks PASS). The iron rule is stated first: falsification conditions written+dated before any measurement; results file to the register whichever way they fall; three named ways the study can REFUTE RPP (models ignore the injection; a malicious counterparty reconstructs; relational recall no better than random). THREE PRE-REGISTERED HYPOTHESES, each with a written falsification: H1 (M1 forcing) — the injected directive forces a CONTENT-BOUND proverb-formation above a no-directive control and survives strip/paraphrase above a canary baseline; falsified if models ignore the directive (f<=0.2), or a trivial strip defeats it, or the proverb is not content-bound (b<=0.5, which would reduce RPP to CoT — the mandatory carve-out made empirical). H2 (Option-C security vs σ) — a malicious INFORMED counterparty (present at the shared experience, holding P_B + the public σ-fraction) cannot reconstruct the withheld (1−σ) of P_A / forge κ; measured as r(σ) over σ∈{0,0.382,0.5,0.618,1} (register C54 candidates + endpoints); falsified if r(0.618)>=0.5 or r(σ) is not monotone in σ (σ not a real knob); a refutation here is the most consequential result and reported at contribution prominence; scope = SEMANTIC layer only, the hard crypto (E11) is assumed not re-tested. H3 (M3 relational recall) — humans regenerate the relational proverb within fuzzy tolerance above matched random mnemonics, gap widening with time; falsified if <= random at any delay or below the recovery threshold at 1 month; usable-security/IRB study sharing design with the WP-11a M3 sweep. THE σ-FRONTIER integrates H2+H3 (they pull opposite ways): the decisive question is whether a σ-band exists where BOTH hold; if none, the σ-tunable construction has no operating point and the paper reports the mechanism non-viable-as-specified (the "t*-of-comprehension" crossover, made concrete). Plus: 7 control arms (E3-C34 precedent), pre-registered power/sample/analysis (per-model not pooled for H1; isotonic monotonicity for H2; mixed-effects for H3; Bonferroni-Holm; analysis code frozen against synthetic ground truth before real data), harness design with ADVERSARIAL INDEPENDENCE (the H2 adversary written by someone other than the interleave author; the content-binding detector validated not black-box), and a DEPLOYMENT-HONESTY statement (interleaved+σ are E3-C32 design-layer; φ values candidate not optima; hard crypto assumed from E11). Scope fences stated (does NOT test auth-primitive claims, does NOT re-test hard crypto, does NOT assert φ optimal, does NOT claim M1 works on all models — the injection-resistant arm bounds it).
EVIDENCE: rehydrations/academic/rpp_adversarial_prereg.md (iron rule §0 + H1/H2/H3 + σ-frontier + arms + power + harness + honesty + fences + handoff); A6 role card (iron rule, WP-11 core, def-of-done); E3-C34 (the multi-arm pre-registration precedent); L163 (Option C + σ ruling); WP-11a (the novelty framing).

(b) FIRST PERSON: commit/date the prereg before any run (the iron rule's commitment step, non-delegable like P4-adjacent freezing) + set the H2 adversary optimisation budget (a policy choice: generous budget makes a passing H2 stronger, a failing H2 honest). [L165][WP-11 / A3][RPP formalism: forcing (behavioral) + withheld-fraction security (conditional reduction) + the layering discipline][2026-07-18][A3 formalist]
FINDING: A3 formalised the ruled RPP construction (rehydrations/academic/rpp_adversarial_formalism.md, tier-A register, 4 checks PASS). The construction: two parties author P_A, P_B of a shared experience E; interleave I combines them; the anchor discloses a σ-fraction A_σ, the withheld (1−σ) fraction W is held jointly; κ = H(I(P_A,P_B)). SIX FORMAL OBJECTS, each proved/cited/labelled-Conjecture-with-missing-step (A3 def-of-done): Def 1 (f,b,s)-FORCING = a BEHAVIORAL property of a reader population under the injected directive, EXPLICITLY not a security guarantee (Remark 1); model-relative because forcing is the DUAL of injection-resistance (Remark 2: f inversely tied to injection-resistance, honest scope carried in the reader population 𝓡; the strongest form is forcing even an injection-resistant population = benchmark arm A3); the content-binding coordinate b separates RPP from CoT (which has b≈0, self-directed), the cooperative-embedded setting separates it from context-bombs. Def 2 interleave (σ,δ)-SPLITTING (I(A_σ;W)≤δ, a measurable property of the map). Def 3 (t,ε)-SECURITY against the malicious informed counterparty (side-info S=(P_B,E,A_σ)) via fuzzy conditional min-entropy H̃_{t,∞}(W|S)≥log(1/ε) (fuzzy-extractor line, DRS2004/JW1999 cited as SUBSTRATE not claimed). Prop 4 (PROVEN-CONDITIONAL) the security REDUCTION ε ≤ 2^{-(m-δ)} where m = H̃_{t,∞}(P_A|P_B,E) — reduces two-party security to TWO MEASURABLE quantities (δ from I, m = residual proverb entropy given a co-participant); TWO NAMED GAPS not hand-waved (interleave-faithfulness requirement; fuzzy-sketch entropy-loss folded into m). Cor 5 (the estimator link) r(σ) estimates ε(σ); the L164 pre-registered falsification r(0.618)≥0.5 IS the formal statement m−δ≤1 bit at σ=0.618 — so H2 measures exactly Prop-4's two inputs. Prop 6 (CONJECTURE, not ∎) monotonicity ε non-increasing as σ decreases IF I is "graceful"; missing step NAMED = gracefulness is a property of a concrete I to be constructed+proven, refutable by H2's isotonic test. THE LAYERING DISCIPLINE (§4, the honesty core): forcing (behavioral, no security) vs security (info-theoretic, holds regardless of reader understanding) must never be conflated — the E6-C06 modal error made concrete. BRAIN-WALLET DISCHARGE MADE PRECISE: security is not one proverb's min-entropy but H̃(P_A|P_B,E) — A's residual entropy given a co-participant's COMPLETE knowledge; the critique is relocated to a measured quantity (m), the honest threat is the informed co-participant, the claim is exactly "m−δ large at the operating σ." FIREWALL §5 (E5 discipline): Prop 4 proved-conditional; m/δ/f/b/s empirical; interleave-faithfulness+gracefulness+fuzzy-loss = engineering requirements listed not assumed; Prop 6 conjecture; φ σ-values conjectural C54; hard crypto assumed from E11; NOTHING unconditionally secure. Coherent-with-not-identical-to WP-07 Cor 5.4b (same informed-adversary conditional-entropy family, distinct objects — B_t accumulating linkage vs S a fixed co-participant; GR-1/GR-4).
EVIDENCE: rehydrations/academic/rpp_adversarial_formalism.md (Defs 1-3, Props 4/6, Cor 5, §4 layering, §5 firewall, §6 handoff); A3 role card (def-of-done: proved/cited/Conjecture-with-missing-step; no % at tier-A; no hand-waving); WP-07 linear_cap_paper Cor 5.4b (the informed-adversary house pattern); L163 (Option C + σ); L164 (the benchmark whose metrics these definitions fix).
PROPOSED: (a) FIRST-PERSON CONSTRUCTION DECISION (new, separate from the L163 architecture ruling): a CONCRETE interleave I must be chosen and shown faithful (Prop 4 gap i) + graceful (Prop 6) — until then the security is a template, not a theorem; this is the load-bearing remaining math. (b) A6: add a direct δ(σ)=I(A_σ;W) measurement so BOTH of Prop 4's inputs are measured (currently m is measured, δ inferred); no other benchmark change. (c) A4: cite DRS2004 + JW1999 at primary records (already in the WP-11a spine) + confirm the fuzzy conditional min-entropy notation matches source. (d) A5-pc (post-results) pre-reads for the two named Prop-4 gaps + that Prop 6 is never stated as proved. (e) the paper's formal spine is now: forcing (Def 1, H1-measured) + the conditional security reduction (Prop 4, H2-measured) + the σ-frontier (H2×H3) — WP-11 is A2-draftable on this skeleton once an I is fixed. Nothing run/committed/pushed.
STATUS: open(A3 formalism drafted, 4 checks PASS; security reduced to 2 measured quantities under 2 named requirements; monotonicity a labelled conjecture; open = First-Person choice of a concrete faithful+graceful interleave I)

[L166][WP-11 / A3 / SOURCES][concrete interleave I* RULED; security reduces to one empirical unknown; the σ-trade grounded in the understanding-as-key "dance not stance" thesis][2026-07-18][First Person ruling ("go for I*"; "dance not stance"); A3 executing]
FINDING: The First Person ruled the concrete interleave: I* = extract-then-block-interleave (over the all-or-nothing alternative), the graded path. Added to the formalism as Definition 7 + Theorem 8 (rpp_adversarial_formalism.md, 4 checks PASS). I* = (1) secure-sketch + strong extractor each proverb (fuzzy tolerance + whiten NL redundancy to near-uniform bits, helper public), (2) symmetric block-interleave to Z, (3) disclose a σ-fraction as the anchor, withhold (1−σ), κ = H(Z). WHAT CHOOSING I* DISCHARGES — the two named gaps of Prop 4/6 collapse into the extractor's STANDARD guarantee, not assumed: SPLITTING δ ≤ δ_ext (bounded extractor uniformity loss, not empirical; a co-participant re-derives the s_B blocks from P_B so they leak nothing, and s_A blocks are near-uniform so σ-disclosure leaks ~0 about the withheld (1−σ)); FAITHFULNESS discharged by the symmetric schedule (W contains (1−σ) of s_A); GRACEFULNESS PROVEN for I* (uniform independent blocks, each withheld adds full entropy) — so Prop 6, a Conjecture for arbitrary I, is a Proposition for I*. THEOREM 8 (proven-conditional): ε ≤ 2^{−((1−σ)·m − δ_ext)}, m = H̃_{t,∞}(P_A | P_B, E) post-sketch, monotone in σ. THE SINGLE REMAINING EMPIRICAL UNKNOWN is m — the residual entropy of a proverb given a co-participant who was present; the entire security question collapses to "how guessable is A's proverb to someone who was there" = exactly benchmark H2. A low-m proverb is insecure under any interleave; I* guarantees you retain (1−σ)·m, no less. TWO REJECTS RECORDED: naive text interleave (δ large, NL redundancy leaks — usable only as the readable surface), AONT (δ=0 maximal but σ binary — kills the graded knob). LAYERING: I* whitens to bits for the SECURITY layer (Z); the human-readable proverb is a SEPARATE surface (M2/M3); σ applies in lockstep (sharing more readable knowledge genuinely lowers security = the σ-frontier, not cosmetic).
